#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

The force calculation sums interactions in body order using double precision. I’ll preserve that order in tiled CUDA kernels, distribute bodies across MPI ranks, and use OpenMP to drive each rank’s GPUs concurrently. The available system has four GPUs, so I can test both a single process using all GPUs and multiple MPI ranks.

 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('nbody/nbody.cpp')
p.write_text(r'''#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <climits>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>
#include <omp.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;
constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 128;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};
static_assert(sizeof(Vec3) == 3 * sizeof(double), \"MPI position layout\");

void checkMPI(int error, const char* operation) {
    if (error == MPI_SUCCESS) return;
    char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
    int length = 0;
    MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
    std::fprintf(stderr, \"%s: %.*s\\n\", operation, length, message);
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, error);
    std::abort();
}

void checkCUDA(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
    std::fprintf(stderr, \"%s: %s\\n\", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    // A failed worker must terminate the job rather than leave peers waiting.
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    std::abort();
}
#define MPI_CHECK(call) checkMPI((call), #call)
#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCUDA((call), #call)

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    // Preserve the original rand_r sequence, including its velocity draws.
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

// Each thread owns one target body. Shared source tiles reduce global loads;
// the j loop remains ordered, including self interactions and softening.
// Separate position buffers keep every force dependent on the old positions.
__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE)
void advanceBodies(const Vec3* __restrict__ positions,
                   Vec3* __restrict__ nextPositions,
                   Vec3* __restrict__ velocities,
                   int numBodies, int offset, int count) {
    __shared__ Vec3 tile[TILE_SIZE];
    const size_t local = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
    const bool active = local < size_t(count);
    const size_t body = size_t(offset) + local;
    const Vec3 p = active ? positions[body] : Vec3{0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(numBodies); base += TILE_SIZE) {
        const size_t source = base + threadIdx.x;
        if (source < size_t(numBodies)) tile[threadIdx.x] = positions[source];
        __syncthreads();
        const int limit = int(size_t(numBodies) - base < TILE_SIZE
                            ? size_t(numBodies) - base : TILE_SIZE);
        if (active) {
            #pragma unroll 4
            for (int j = 0; j < limit; ++j) {
                const double dx = tile[j].x - p.x;
                const double dy = tile[j].y - p.y;
                const double dz = tile[j].z - p.z;
                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
                fx += dx * invDist3;
                fy += dy * invDist3;
                fz += dz * invDist3;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    if (active) {
        Vec3 v = velocities[local];
        v.x += DT * fx;
        v.y += DT * fy;
        v.z += DT * fz;
        velocities[local] = v;
        nextPositions[body] = {p.x + v.x * DT, p.y + v.y * DT, p.z + v.z * DT};
    }
}

// Diagnostic energy is also distributed. Each i<j pair is counted once.
__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE)
void bodyEnergies(const Vec3* __restrict__ positions,
                  const Vec3* __restrict__ velocities,
                  double* __restrict__ energies, int numBodies, int offset, int count) {
    __shared__ Vec3 tile[TILE_SIZE];
    const size_t local = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
    const bool active = local < size_t(count);
    const size_t body = size_t(offset) + local;
    const Vec3 p = active ? positions[body] : Vec3{0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
    const Vec3 v = active ? velocities[local] : Vec3{0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
    double energy = 0.5 * (v.x * v.x + v.y * v.y + v.z * v.z);
    const size_t first = (size_t(offset) + size_t(blockIdx.x) * TILE_SIZE) / TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE;
    for (size_t base = first; base < size_t(numBodies); base += TILE_SIZE) {
        const size_t source = base + threadIdx.x;
        if (source < size_t(numBodies)) tile[threadIdx.x] = positions[source];
        __syncthreads();
        const int limit = int(size_t(numBodies) - base < TILE_SIZE
                            ? size_t(numBodies) - base : TILE_SIZE);
        if (active) {
            #pragma unroll 4
            for (int j = 0; j < limit; ++j) {
                if (base + j > body) {
                    const double dx = tile[j].x - p.x;
                    const double dy = tile[j].y - p.y;
                    const double dz = tile[j].z - p.z;
                    energy -= 1.0 / sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
                }
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    if (active) energies[local] = energy;
}

struct GPU {
    int device = 0, offset = 0, count = 0, current = 0;
    Vec3* positions[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
    Vec3* velocities = nullptr;
    double* energies = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t ready = nullptr;
};

int hostThreads(int count) {
    // Avoid starting a large CPU team for tiny packing/validation jobs.
    return std::max(1, std::min(omp_get_max_threads(), count / 8192));
}

bool parseNumber(const char* text, int& number) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    errno = 0;
    const long value = std::strtol(text, &end, 10);
    if (errno || end == text || *end || value < 0 || value > INT_MAX) return false;
    number = int(value);
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks finite states and reports energy)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int run(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int ranks) {
    int numBodies = 1024, numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false, printResults = false;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if ((strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 || strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0) && i + 1 < argc) {
            const bool bodiesOption = strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0;
            if ("'!parseNumber(argv[++i], bodiesOption ? numBodies : numSteps)) {
                if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Invalid nonnegative integer: %s'"\\n\", argv[i]);
                return 1;
            }
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            return 1;
        }
    }
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");
        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);
        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);
        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    }

    MPI_Comm node;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
    int localRank = 0, localRanks = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(node, &localRanks));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
    int deviceCount = 0;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
    if (deviceCount == 0) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: the hybrid solver requires a CUDA device\\n\", rank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    }

    // Respect per-rank CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES and use all visible GPUs when fewer
    // ranks than GPUs are launched. Excess ranks share devices round-robin.
    std::vector<GPU> gpus;
    if (localRanks <= deviceCount) {
        for (int d = localRank; d < deviceCount; d += localRanks) {
            GPU gpu;
            gpu.device = d;
            gpus.push_back(gpu);
        }
    } else {
        GPU gpu;
        gpu.device = localRank % deviceCount;
        gpus.push_back(gpu);
    }
    const int workers = int(gpus.size());
    std::vector<int> gpuCounts(ranks), counts(ranks), offsets(ranks);
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allgather(&workers, 1, MPI_INT, gpuCounts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    long long totalGPUs = 0, firstGPU = 0;
    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
        if (r == rank) firstGPU = totalGPUs;
        totalGPUs += gpuCounts[r];
    }
    // Weight the decomposition by GPU count, including heterogeneous nodes.
    auto boundary = [&](long long gpu) { return int((long long(numBodies) * gpu) / totalGPUs); };
    long long cursor = 0;
    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
        offsets[r] = boundary(cursor);
        cursor += gpuCounts[r];
        counts[r] = boundary(cursor) - offsets[r];
    }
    for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
        gpus[w].offset = boundary(firstGPU + w);
        gpus[w].count = boundary(firstGPU + w + 1) - gpus[w].offset;
    }
    const int localCount = counts[rank], localOffset = offsets[rank];
    MPI_Datatype positionType;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &positionType));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_commit(&positionType));

    // Pinned double buffers allow asynchronous copies without overwriting a
    // buffer that another GPU is still reading during the previous upload.
    const size_t positionBytes = size_t(numBodies) * sizeof(Vec3);
    Vec3* hostPositions[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
    Vec3* hostVelocities = nullptr;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostPositions[0], std::max(positionBytes, sizeof(Vec3)), cudaHostAllocPortable));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostPositions[1], std::max(positionBytes, sizeof(Vec3)), cudaHostAllocPortable));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostVelocities, size_t(std::max(1, localCount)) * sizeof(Vec3), cudaHostAllocPortable));
    std::vector<Body> bodies;
    std::vector<Vec3> allVelocities;
    if (rank == 0) {
        bodies.resize(numBodies);
        allVelocities.resize(numBodies);
        randomizeBodies(bodies);
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(hostThreads(numBodies))
        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {
            hostPositions[0][i] = bodies[i].pos;
            allVelocities[i] = bodies[i].vel;
        }
        if ("'!printResults) std::vector<Body>().swap(bodies);
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(hostPositions[0], numBodies, positionType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Scatterv(allVelocities.data(), counts.data(), offsets.data(), positionType,
                         hostVelocities, localCount, positionType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    if (!printResults) std::vector<Vec3>().swap(allVelocities);

    // One persistent OpenMP worker and one nonblocking CUDA stream per GPU.
    // MPI collectives in the simulation are executed only by the master.
    omp_set_dynamic(0);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static, 1) num_threads(workers)
    for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
        GPU& gpu = gpus[w];
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&gpu.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&gpu.ready, cudaEventDisableTiming));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.positions[0], std::max(positionBytes, sizeof(Vec3))));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.positions[1], std::max(positionBytes, sizeof(Vec3))));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.velocities, size_t(std::max(1, gpu.count)) * sizeof(Vec3)));
        if (validate) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.energies, size_t(std::max(1, gpu.count)) * sizeof(double)));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(gpu.positions[0], hostPositions[0], positionBytes,
                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(gpu.velocities, hostVelocities + gpu.offset - localOffset,
                                   size_t(gpu.count) * sizeof(Vec3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
        if (ranks == 1) {
            for (const auto& other : gpus) {
                if (other.device == gpu.device) continue;
                int accessible = 0;
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceCanAccessPeer(&accessible, gpu.device, other.device));
                if (accessible) {
                    const cudaError_t error = cudaDeviceEnablePeerAccess(other.device, 0);
                    if (error == cudaErrorPeerAccessAlreadyEnabled) cudaGetLastError();
                    else CUDA_CHECK(error);
                }
            }
        }
        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, advanceBodies));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    }

    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)
    {
        GPU& gpu = gpus[omp_get_thread_num()];
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
            const int next = 1 - gpu.current;
            if (gpu.count > 0) {
                advanceBodies<<<(gpu.count - 1) / TILE_SIZE + 1, TILE_SIZE, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                    gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.positions[next], gpu.velocities,
                    numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
            }
            if (step + 1 < numSteps && (ranks > 1 || workers > 1)) {
                if (ranks == 1) {
                    // Device-to-device exchange avoids host staging within a
                    // process. CUDA stages inaccessible peer links itself.
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.ready, gpu.stream));
                    #pragma omp barrier
                    for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
                        const GPU& source = gpus[w];
                        if (w == omp_get_thread_num() || source.count == 0) continue;
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.stream, source.ready, 0));
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyPeerAsync(gpu.positions[next] + source.offset, gpu.device,
                            source.positions[next] + source.offset, source.device,
                            size_t(source.count) * sizeof(Vec3), gpu.stream));
                    }
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
                    // All remote reads must finish before reusing a buffer.
                    #pragma omp barrier
                } else {
                    Vec3* exchange = hostPositions[(step + 1) & 1];
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(exchange + gpu.offset, gpu.positions[next] + gpu.offset,
                        size_t(gpu.count) * sizeof(Vec3), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
                    #pragma omp barrier
                    #pragma omp master
                    {
                        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, positionType, exchange,
                            counts.data(), offsets.data(), positionType, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
                    }
                    #pragma omp barrier
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(gpu.positions[next], exchange, positionBytes,
                        cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
                }
            }
            gpu.current = next;
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    }
    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double maximumElapsed = 0.0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &maximumElapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    if (rank == 0) printf("Simulation time: %lld ms'"\\n\", static_cast<long long>(maximumElapsed * 1000.0));

    // Velocities stay on their owner GPU throughout the simulation; only
    // requested output/validation transfers them and the final positions.
    if (printResults || validate) {
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static, 1) num_threads(workers)
        for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
            const GPU& gpu = gpus[w];
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostPositions[0] + gpu.offset, gpu.positions[gpu.current] + gpu.offset,
                size_t(gpu.count) * sizeof(Vec3), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostVelocities + gpu.offset - localOffset, gpu.velocities,
                size_t(gpu.count) * sizeof(Vec3), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
        }
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, positionType, hostPositions[0],
            counts.data(), offsets.data(), positionType, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    }
    if (printResults) {
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(hostVelocities, localCount, positionType, allVelocities.data(),
            counts.data(), offsets.data(), positionType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        if (rank == 0) {
            std::vector<double> bodyData(size_t(numBodies) * 6);
            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(hostThreads(numBodies))
            for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {
                const Vec3 p = hostPositions[0][i], v = allVelocities[i];
                const size_t index = size_t(i) * 6;
                bodyData[index] = p.x;
                bodyData[index + 1] = p.y;
                bodyData[index + 2] = p.z;
                bodyData[index + 3] = v.x;
                bodyData[index + 4] = v.y;
                bodyData[index + 5] = v.z;
            }
            print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");
        }
    }

    int result = 0;
    if (validate) {
        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");
        // Preserve the original first failing body's diagnostic.
        unsigned long long failure = std::numeric_limits<unsigned long long>::max();
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(hostThreads(localCount)) reduction(min:failure)
        for (int i = 0; i < localCount; ++i) {
            const Vec3 p = hostPositions[0][localOffset + i], v = hostVelocities[i];
            int reason = -1;
            if ("'!std::isfinite(p.x) || !std::isfinite(p.y) || !std::isfinite(p.z) ||
                !std::isfinite(v.x) || !std::isfinite(v.y) || !std::isfinite(v.z)) reason = 0;
            else if (std::abs(p.x) > 1e6 || std::abs(p.y) > 1e6 || std::abs(p.z) > 1e6) reason = 1;
            else if (std::abs(v.x) > 1e6 || std::abs(v.y) > 1e6 || std::abs(v.z) > 1e6) reason = 2;
            if (reason >= 0) failure = std::min(failure, 3ULL * (localOffset + i) + reason);
        }
        unsigned long long globalFailure = 0;
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&failure, &globalFailure, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        if (globalFailure != std::numeric_limits<unsigned long long>::max()) {
            result = 1;
            if (rank == 0) {
                const char* reasons[] = {"found NaN or Inf value in body state",
                    "body position exceeds reasonable bounds", "body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds"};
                printf("Validation failed: %s'"\\n\", reasons[globalFailure % 3]);
                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            }
        } else {
            std::vector<double> energies(localCount);
            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static, 1) num_threads(workers)
            for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
                const GPU& gpu = gpus[w];
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(gpu.positions[gpu.current], hostPositions[0], positionBytes,
                    cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
                if (gpu.count > 0) {
                    bodyEnergies<<<(gpu.count - 1) / TILE_SIZE + 1, TILE_SIZE, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.velocities, gpu.energies, numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(energies.data() + gpu.offset - localOffset, gpu.energies,
                        size_t(gpu.count) * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
                }
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
            }
            double localEnergy = 0.0, finalEnergy = 0.0;
            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(hostThreads(localCount)) reduction(+:localEnergy)
            for (int i = 0; i < localCount; ++i) localEnergy += energies[i];
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localEnergy, &finalEnergy, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);
                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            }
        }
    }

    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static, 1) num_threads(workers)
    for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
        const GPU& gpu = gpus[w];
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.positions[0]));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.positions[1]));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.velocities));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.energies));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(gpu.ready));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(gpu.stream));
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostPositions[0]));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostPositions[1]));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostVelocities));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_free(&positionType));
    return result;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int provided = 0;
    const int error = MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
    if (error "'!= MPI_SUCCESS) return 1;
    if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support is required'"\\n\");
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
    int rank = 0, ranks = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
    int result = 1;
    try {
        result = run(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
    } catch (const std::exception& exception) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: %s\\n\", rank, exception.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
    return result;
}
''')

codex
The implementation is in place. It keeps velocities on their owner GPU, exchanges only positions between steps, and combines force calculation and integration in one kernel. A process using multiple GPUs exchanges positions through CUDA peer copies; MPI ranks use pinned host buffers, so CUDA-aware MPI is not required. I’ll now build it and compare results with the original.

-cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
-project(nbody_benchmark)
+cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)
+
+# Cover common cluster GPUs, with PTX for newer devices. A cluster build can
+# select its own architectures with -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=...
+if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
+    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES 70 80 86)
+endif()
+project(nbody_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
 
 set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
 set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 17)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
+
+find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
+find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
+find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)
 
+# Keep the existing source filename while compiling its CUDA kernels.
+set_source_files_properties(nbody.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
 add_executable(nbody nbody.cpp)
+target_compile_definitions(nbody PRIVATE OMPI_SKIP_MPICXX MPICH_SKIP_MPICXX)

+# Preserve double precision and the original order of each force sum.
+target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE
+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra,-ffp-contract=off>
+)
+separate_arguments(NBODY_OPENMP_FLAGS NATIVE_COMMAND "${OpenMP_CXX_FLAGS}")
+foreach(flag IN LISTS NBODY_OPENMP_FLAGS)

+endforeach()

-target_link_libraries(nbody m)
+target_link_libraries(nbody PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX CUDA::cudart m)

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u - <<'PY'
import os, re, subprocess
binary = '/tmp/nbody-hybrid-build/nbody'
cases = [(0, 3, 4), (1, 5, 4), (3, 3, 4), (129, 0, 2), (131, 7, 2), (257, 4, 4)]
for n, steps, ranks in cases:
    args = ['-n', str(n), '-s', str(steps), '-r', '-v']
    ref = subprocess.run(['/tmp/nbody-original-strict'] + args, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    got = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--allow-run-as-root', '--bind-to', 'none', '-np', str(ranks), binary] + args, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90)
    expected = ref.stdout.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0]
    actual = got.stdout.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0] if '=== RESULTS ===' in got.stdout else got.stdout
    assert got.returncode == ref.returncode and expected == actual, (n, steps, ranks, ref.stdout, got.stdout, got.stderr)
    assert 'Validation: PASSED' in got.stdout
    print(f'PASS: bodies={n}, steps={steps}, MPI ranks={ranks}: exact serial result statistics/hash')
for visible in ['0', '0,1']:
    env = dict(os.environ, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=visible)
    got = subprocess.run([binary, '-n', '131', '-s', '7', '-r', '-v'], env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90, check=True)
    ref = subprocess.run(['/tmp/nbody-original-strict', '-n', '131', '-s', '7', '-r', '-v'], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    assert got.stdout.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0] == ref.stdout.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0]
    print(f'PASS: CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={visible}: exact serial result')

    ('serial original', ['/tmp/nbody-original-native'], {}),
    ('one GPU', ['/tmp/nbody-hybrid-build/nbody'], {'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES':'0'}),

    print(name, '\\n' + '\\n'.join(selected), flush=True)

codex
The tested configurations—one, two, and four MPI ranks, plus one process using multiple GPUs—produce identical result hashes to the original compiled without fused floating-point contractions. Edge cases also pass, including empty inputs, zero steps, uneven partitions, and more ranks than bodies. For 16,384 bodies and three steps, I measured about 5,978 ms for the original and 65 ms with four GPUs. I’ll now tune GPU occupancy and check OpenMP thread limits.

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('nbody/nbody.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 128;\\n', '')
start=s.index('__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE)')
end=s.index('\\nstruct GPU {', start)
part=s[start:end].replace('TILE_SIZE', 'TileSize')
part=part.replace('__global__ __launch_bounds__(TileSize)', 'template<int TileSize>\\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TileSize)')
s=s[:start]+part+s[end:]
s=s.replace('int device = 0, offset = 0, count = 0, current = 0;', 'int device = 0, offset = 0, count = 0, current = 0, tileSize = 128;')
s=s.replace('    Vec3* hostVelocities = nullptr;\\n', '    Vec3* hostVelocities = nullptr;\\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpus.front().device));\\n')
s=s.replace('    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostPositions[1], std::max(positionBytes, sizeof(Vec3)), cudaHostAllocPortable));', '    if (ranks > 1) CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostPositions[1], std::max(positionBytes, sizeof(Vec3)), cudaHostAllocPortable));')
s=s.replace('        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;\\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, advanceBodies));', '''        cudaDeviceProp properties;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, gpu.device));
        // Small partitions need more blocks to occupy all SMs. Larger tiles
        // improve source reuse once there are enough targets for occupancy.
        gpu.tileSize = gpu.count < properties.multiProcessorCount * 64 * 4 ? 32
                     : gpu.count < properties.multiProcessorCount * 128 * 4 ? 64 : 128;
        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, advanceBodies<32>));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, advanceBodies<64>));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, advanceBodies<128>));''')
start=s.index('    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)\\n')
end=s.index('    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;', start)
s=s[:start]+'''    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)
    {
        // Worksharing also handles an OpenMP runtime limiting the team to
        // fewer threads than GPUs; every GPU still receives its work.
        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
            #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1)
            for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
                GPU& gpu = gpus[w];
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
                if (gpu.count > 0) launchAdvance(gpu, numBodies);
                if (step + 1 < numSteps && (ranks > 1 || workers > 1)) {
                    if (ranks == 1) {
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.ready, gpu.stream));
                    } else {
                        Vec3* exchange = hostPositions[(step + 1) & 1];
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(exchange + gpu.offset,
                            gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current] + gpu.offset,
                            size_t(gpu.count) * sizeof(Vec3), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
                    }
                }
            }
            if (step + 1 < numSteps && (ranks > 1 || workers > 1)) {
                if (ranks > 1) {
                    #pragma omp master
                    {
                        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, positionType,
                            hostPositions[(step + 1) & 1], counts.data(), offsets.data(),
                            positionType, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
                    }
                    #pragma omp barrier
                }
                #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1)
                for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
                    GPU& gpu = gpus[w];
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpu.device));
                    if (ranks == 1) {
                        // CUDA stages inaccessible peer links itself.
                        for (int sourceIndex = 0; sourceIndex < workers; ++sourceIndex) {
                            const GPU& source = gpus[sourceIndex];
                            if (sourceIndex == w || source.count == 0) continue;
                            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.stream, source.ready, 0));
                            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyPeerAsync(
                                gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current] + source.offset, gpu.device,
                                source.positions[1 - source.current] + source.offset, source.device,
                                size_t(source.count) * sizeof(Vec3), gpu.stream));
                        }
                        // All remote reads finish before buffers are reused.
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
                    } else {
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current],
                            hostPositions[(step + 1) & 1], positionBytes,
                            cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
                    }
                }
            }
            #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1)
            for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) gpus[w].current = 1 - gpus[w].current;
        }
        #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1)
        for (int w = 0; w < workers; ++w) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(gpus[w].device));
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpus[w].stream));
        }
    }
'''+s[end:]
s=s.replace('''                    bodyEnergies<<<(gpu.count - 1) / TILE_SIZE + 1, TILE_SIZE, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.velocities, gpu.energies, numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());''', '''                    launchEnergies(gpu, numBodies);''')
s=s.replace('    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostPositions[1]));', '    if (hostPositions[1]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostPositions[1]));')
pos=s.index('\\nint hostThreads(')
s=s[:pos]+'''
template<int TileSize>
void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
    advanceBodies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current], gpu.velocities,
        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
}

void launchAdvance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) advance<32>(gpu, numBodies);
    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) advance<64>(gpu, numBodies);
    else advance<128>(gpu, numBodies);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
}

template<int TileSize>
void energy(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
    bodyEnergies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.velocities, gpu.energies,
        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
}

void launchEnergies(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) energy<32>(gpu, numBodies);
    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) energy<64>(gpu, numBodies);
    else energy<128>(gpu, numBodies);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
}
''' +s[pos:]
p.write_text(s)

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)

# Cover common cluster GPUs, with PTX for newer devices. A cluster build can
# select its own architectures with -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=...
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES 70 80 86)
endif()
project(nbody_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)

# Keep the existing source filename while compiling its CUDA kernels.
set_source_files_properties(nbody.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
add_executable(nbody nbody.cpp)
target_compile_definitions(nbody PRIVATE OMPI_SKIP_MPICXX MPICH_SKIP_MPICXX)

# Preserve double precision and the original order of each force sum.
target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra,-ffp-contract=off>
)
separate_arguments(NBODY_OPENMP_FLAGS NATIVE_COMMAND "${OpenMP_CXX_FLAGS}")
foreach(flag IN LISTS NBODY_OPENMP_FLAGS)
    target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=${flag}>)
endforeach()

target_link_libraries(nbody PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX CUDA::cudart m)

+void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {

+template<int TileSize>
+void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    advanceBodies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current], gpu.velocities,
+        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
+}
+
+void launchAdvance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) advance<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) advance<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else advance<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+template<int TileSize>
+void energy(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    bodyEnergies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.velocities, gpu.energies,
+        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
+}
+
+void launchEnergies(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) energy<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) energy<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else energy<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+template<int TileSize>
+void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    advanceBodies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current], gpu.velocities,

+
+void launchAdvance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) advance<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) advance<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else advance<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}

codex
Smaller CUDA tiles improved the four-GPU time to about 38 ms for the same 16,384-body workload. Results remain identical across the tested MPI and GPU configurations, including an OpenMP team limited to one thread. The default CMake build also succeeds with support for several common GPU architectures. I’m finishing with a larger scaling run and final edge-case checks.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u - <<'PY'
import os, re, subprocess
binary='/tmp/nbody-hybrid-default-build/nbody'
args=['-n','65536','-s','2','-r']
expected=None
for name, command, extra in [
    ('one GPU', [binary], {'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES':'0'}),
    ('four GPUs, one rank', [binary], {}),
    ('four GPUs, four MPI ranks', ['mpirun','--allow-run-as-root','--bind-to','none','-np','4',binary], {}),
]:
    got=subprocess.run(command+args,env=dict(os.environ,**extra),capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=120,check=True)
    data=got.stdout.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0]
    if expected is None: expected=data
    assert data==expected, (name, expected, data)
    print(f'65536 bodies / 2 steps / {name}: '+next(line for line in got.stdout.splitlines() if line.startswith('Simulation time:'))+'; identical result hash')
for n, steps, ranks, limit in [(0,3,4,'1'),(3,4,2,'1'),(129,0,2,'2'),(131,7,1,'1')]:
    args=['-n',str(n),'-s',str(steps),'-r','-v']

    command=['mpirun','--allow-run-as-root','--bind-to','none','-np',str(ranks),binary] if ranks>1 else [binary]

+template<int TileSize>
+void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    advanceBodies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current], gpu.velocities,
+        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
+}
+
+void launchAdvance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) advance<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) advance<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else advance<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+template<int TileSize>
+void energy(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    bodyEnergies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.velocities, gpu.energies,
+        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
+}
+
+void launchEnergies(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) energy<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) energy<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else energy<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}

+template<int TileSize>
+void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    advanceBodies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.positions[1 - gpu.current], gpu.velocities,
+        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
+}
+
+void launchAdvance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) advance<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) advance<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else advance<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+template<int TileSize>
+void energy(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    bodyEnergies<TileSize><<<(gpu.count - 1) / TileSize + 1, TileSize, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
+        gpu.positions[gpu.current], gpu.velocities, gpu.energies,
+        numBodies, gpu.offset, gpu.count);
+}
+
+void launchEnergies(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {
+    if (gpu.tileSize == 32) energy<32>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else if (gpu.tileSize == 64) energy<64>(gpu, numBodies);
+    else energy<128>(gpu, numBodies);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}

+template<int TileSize>
+void advance(const GPU& gpu, int numBodies) {

codex
Implemented MPI body partitioning, OpenMP GPU coordination, and tiled CUDA kernels combining force calculation and integration. CMake requires all three technologies; the executable remains `nbody`. Only the two existing files were changed.

Verified builds, edge cases, and 1–4 MPI ranks. Results exactly match the serial reference with FMA disabled; FMA-enabled builds can produce different floating-point hashes.

For 65,536 bodies over two steps, simulation time improved from **1,047 ms on one GPU to 296 ms on four GPUs**.

tokens used
94,965
Implemented MPI body partitioning, OpenMP GPU coordination, and tiled CUDA kernels combining force calculation and integration. CMake requires all three technologies; the executable remains `nbody`. Only the two existing files were changed.

Verified builds, edge cases, and 1–4 MPI ranks. Results exactly match the serial reference with FMA disabled; FMA-enabled builds can produce different floating-point hashes.

For 65,536 bodies over two steps, simulation time improved from **1,047 ms on one GPU to 296 ms on four GPUs**.
